DATA PROCESSING AGREEMENT
Last updated 29 th  November 2024
This Data Processing Agreement ('DPA', 'Agreement') is an addendum to and forms part of the master agreement between the Administrator of Bitrix24 Customer Account and Alaio (https://www.bitrix24.com/terms/) to reflect the parties' agreement for the provision of the Processor Services (as amended from time to time) and processing of Bitrix24 Customer Account User's Personal Data in accordance with the requirements of the Data Protection Legislation.
This Data Processing Agreement will be effective from the Effective Date.
BY CLICKING THE "I ACCEPT" BUTTON BELOW, YOU (A) ACKNOWLEDGE THAT YOU HAVE READ AND UNDERSTAND THIS AGREEMENT; (B) REPRESENT AND WARRANT THAT YOU HAVE THE RIGHT, POWER, AND AUTHORITY TO ENTER INTO THIS AGREEMENT; AND (C) ACCEPT THIS AGREEMENT AND AGREE THAT YOU ARE LEGALLY BOUND BY ITS TERMS.

(b) Networks & Transmission .
Data Transmission . Alaio Datacenters are connected via private links protected by AWS Network firewalls to provide secure data transfer. This is designed to protect the confidentiality, integrity and availability of the network and prevent data from being read, copied, altered or removed without authorization during electronic transfer.
Data Breach Response . Alaio monitors a variety of communication channels for security breaches, and Alaio  security personnel will react promptly to known incidents.
External Attack Surface . Alaio considers potential attack vectors and incorporates appropriate purpose built proprietary technologies into external facing systems.
Encryption Technologies . Alaio uses HTTPS encryption (also referred to as SSL or TLS connection).

seed S135 document.appendix_1.appendix_2.section_governance_risk

spanId=doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4::section::42887-43935 · chars=[42887, 43935) · scope=unspecified · doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4 · document.appendix_1.appendix_2.section_governance_risk

GOVERNANCE & RISK
Data Center risk management. The AWS Security Operations Center performs regular threat and vulnerability reviews of data centers. Ongoing assessment and mitigation of potential vulnerabilities is performed through data center risk assessment activities. This assessment is performed in addition to the enterprise-level risk assessment process used to identify and manage risks presented to the business as a whole. This process also takes regional regulatory and environmental risks into consideration.
Third-party security attestation. Third-party testing of AWS data centers, as documented in our third-party reports, ensures AWS has appropriately implemented security measures aligned to established rules needed to obtain security certifications. Depending on the compliance program and its requirements, external auditors may perform testing of media disposal, review security camera footage, observe entrances and hallways throughout a data center, test electronic access control devices, and examine data center equipment.

3.2 Specific for data transfers from EU, UK and Switzerland to the USA, Alaio, Inc complies with the EU-U.S. Data Privacy Framework (EU-U.S. DPF), the UK Extension to the EU-U.S. DPF, and the Swiss-U.S. Data Privacy Framework (SwissU.S. DPF) as set forth by the U.S. Department of Commerce.  Alaio, Inc has certified to the U.S. Department of Commerce that it adheres to the EU-U.S. Data Privacy Framework Principles (EU-U.S. DPF Principles) with regard to the processing of personal data received from the European Union in reliance on the EU-U.S. DPF and from the United Kingdom (and Gibraltar) in reliance on the UK Extension to the EU-U.S. DPF.  Alaio, Inc has certified to the U.S. Department of Commerce that it adheres to the Swiss-U.S. Data Privacy Framework Principles (Swiss-U.S. DPF Principles) with regard to the processing of personal data received from Switzerland in reliance on the Swiss-U.S. DPF.  If there is any conflict between the terms in this DPA and the EU-U.S. DPF Principles and/or the Swiss-U.S. DPF Principles, the Principles shall govern.  To learn more about the Data Privacy Framework (DPF) program, and to view our certification, please
visit https://www.dataprivacyframework.gov/. The Data Privacy Framework (DPF) list of participating organisations is available at https://www.dataprivacyframework.gov/s/participant-search. Under certain conditions, you can invoke the corresponding binding arbitration.

spanId=doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4::section::13363-14205 · chars=[13363, 14205) · scope=unspecified · doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4 · document.clause_4.subclause_4.2

4.2 Alaio shall, to the extent legally permitted, promptly notify Administrator if it receives a request from a Data Subject for access to, correction, amendment, restriction, deletion or exercising any other rights under the GDPR or other Data Protection Legislation of that person's Personal Data. Alaio shall not respond to any such Data Subject request without Administrator´s  prior written consent except to confirm that the request relates to User. Alaio shall provide Administrator with cooperation and assistance in relation to handling of a Data Sub ject's request for access to that person's Personal Data or exercising any other rights under the GDPR or other Data Protection Legislation, to the extent legally permitted and to the extent User does not have access to such Personal Data through its use or receipt of the Services.

4 Objection Right for New Sub-processors.
When any new Sub-processor is engaged during the Term, and if the GDPR applies to the processing of Personal Data, Alaio will, at least 10 days before the new Sub-processor processes any Personal Data, inform Administrator of the engagement (including the name and location of the relevant sub-processor and the activities it will perform) by sending an email to the Notification Email Address. Administrator may object to any new Sub-processor by notifying Alaio promptly in writing within five (5) business days after receipt of Alaio notice. In the event Administrator objects to a new Sub-processor, Alaio will use reasonable efforts to make available to Administrator a change in the Services or recommend a commercially reasonable change to Administrator´s configuration or use of the Services to avoid Processing of Personal Data by the objected-to new Sub-processor without unreasonably burdening the User. If Alaio is unable to make available such change within a reasonable period of time, which shall not exceed thirty (30) days, Administrator may terminate the applicable Order with respect only to those Services which cannot be provided by Alaio without the use of the objected-to new Sub-processor by providing written notice to Alaio. Alaio will refund Administrator any prepaid fees covering the remainder of the term of such Order following the effective date of termination with respect to such terminated Services, without imposing a pena

9. SUBPROCESSOR SECURITY
Before onboarding Sub-processors, Alaio conducts an audit of the security and privacy practices of Sub-processors to ensure Sub-processors provide a level of security and privacy appropriate to their access to data and the scope of the services they are engaged to provide. Once Alaio has assessed the risks presented by the Sub-processor then, subject always to the requirements set out in Section 7 the Sub-processor is required to enter into appropriate security, confidentiality and privacy contract terms.

Alaio, Inc has certified to the U.S. Department of Commerce that it adheres to the EU-U.S. Data Privacy Framework Principles (EU-U.S. DPF Principles) with regard to the processing of personal data received from the European Union in reliance on the EU-U.S. DPF and from the United Kingdom (and Gibraltar) in reliance on the UK Extension to the EU-U.S. DPF.  Alaio, Inc has certified to the U.S. Department of Commerce that it adheres to the Swiss-U.S. Data Privacy Framework Principles (Swiss-U.S. DPF Principles) with regard to the processing of personal data received from Switzerland in reliance on the Swiss-U.S. DPF.  If there is any conflict between the terms in this DPA and the EU-U.S. DPF Principles and/or the Swiss-U.S. DPF Principles, the Principles shall govern.  To learn more about the Data Privacy Framework (DPF) program, and to view our certification, please
visit https://www.dataprivacyframework.gov/. The Data Privacy Framework (DPF) list of participating organisations is available at https://www.dataprivacyframework.gov/s/participant-search. Under certain conditions, you can invoke the corresponding binding arbitration.
The Federal Trade Commission has jurisdiction over Alaio, Inc

DATA PROCESSING AGREEMENT
Last updated 29 th  November 2024
This Data Processing Agreement ('DPA', 'Agreement') is an addendum to and forms part of the master agreement between the Administrator of Bitrix24 Customer Account and Alaio (https://www.bitrix24.com/terms/) to reflect the parties' agreement for the provision of the Processor Services (as amended from time to time) and processing of Bitrix24 Customer Account User's Personal Data in accordance with the requirements of the Data Protection Legislation.
This Data Processing Agreement will be effective from the Effective Date.
BY CLICKING THE "I ACCEPT" BUTTON BELOW, YOU (A) ACKNOWLEDGE THAT YOU HAVE READ AND UNDERSTAND THIS AGREEMENT; (B) REPRESENT AND WARRANT THAT YOU HAVE THE RIGHT, POWER, AND AUTHORITY TO ENTER INTO THIS AGREEMENT; AND (C) ACCEPT THIS AGREEMENT AND AGREE THAT YOU ARE LEGALLY BOUND BY ITS TERMS.

seed S1 document.section_data_processing_agreement
spanId=doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4::section::0-881 · chars=[0, 881) · scope=unspecified · doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4 · document.section_data_processing_agreement
DATA PROCESSING AGREEMENT
Last updated 29 th  November 2024
This Data Processing Agreement ('DPA', 'Agreement') is an addendum to and forms part of the master agreement between the Administrator of Bitrix24 Customer Account and Alaio (https://www.bitrix24.com/terms/) to reflect the parties' agreement for the provision of the Processor Services (as amended from time to time) and processing of Bitrix24 Customer Account User's Personal Data in accordance with the requirements of the Data Protection Legislation.
This Data Processing Agreement will be effective from the Effective Date.
BY CLICKING THE "I ACCEPT" BUTTON BELOW, YOU (A) ACKNOWLEDGE THAT YOU HAVE READ AND UNDERSTAND THIS AGREEMENT; (B) REPRESENT AND WARRANT THAT YOU HAVE THE RIGHT, POWER, AND AUTHORITY TO ENTER INTO THIS AGREEMENT; AND (C) ACCEPT THIS AGREEMENT AND AGREE THAT YOU ARE LEGALLY BOUND BY ITS TERMS.

GDPR.
The supervisory authority of the single establishment of Alaio within the EEA competent to act  as  lead  supervisory  authority  for  the  cross-border  processing  carried  out  by  that processor in accordance with the procedure provided in Article 60 of GDPR is the supervisory authority of Cyprus.

seed S135 document.appendix_1.appendix_2.section_governance_risk
spanId=doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4::section::42887-43935 · chars=[42887, 43935) · scope=unspecified · doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4 · document.appendix_1.appendix_2.section_governance_risk
GOVERNANCE & RISK
Data Center risk management. The AWS Security Operations Center performs regular threat and vulnerability reviews of data centers. Ongoing assessment and mitigation of potential vulnerabilities is performed through data center risk assessment activities. This assessment is performed in addition to the enterprise-level risk assessment process used to identify and manage risks presented to the business as a whole. This process also takes regional regulatory and environmental risks into consideration.
Third-party security attestation. Third-party testing of AWS data centers, as documented in our third-party reports, ensures AWS has appropriately implemented security measures aligned to established rules needed to obtain security certifications. Depending on the compliance program and its requirements, external auditors may perform testing of media disposal, review security camera footage, observe entrances and hallways throughout a data center, test electronic access control devices, and examine data center equipment.

seed S136 document.appendix_1.appendix_2.list_item_(b)
spanId=doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4::section::43936-44736 · chars=[43936, 44736) · scope=unspecified · doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4 · document.appendix_1.appendix_2.list_item_(b)
(b) Networks & Transmission .
Data Transmission . Alaio Datacenters are connected via private links protected by AWS Network firewalls to provide secure data transfer. This is designed to protect the confidentiality, integrity and availability of the network and prevent data from being read, copied, altered or removed without authorization during electronic transfer.
Data Breach Response . Alaio monitors a variety of communication channels for security breaches, and Alaio  security personnel will react promptly to known incidents.
External Attack Surface . Alaio considers potential attack vectors and incorporates appropriate purpose built proprietary technologies into external facing systems.
Encryption Technologies . Alaio uses HTTPS encryption (also referred to as SSL or TLS connection).


seed S51 document.list_item_(c)~3.list_item_74
spanId=doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4::section::17797-19339 · chars=[17797, 19339) · scope=unspecified · doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4 · document.list_item_(c)~3.list_item_74
4 Objection Right for New Sub-processors.
When any new Sub-processor is engaged during the Term, and if the GDPR applies to the processing of Personal Data, Alaio will, at least 10 days before the new Sub-processor processes any Personal Data, inform Administrator of the engagement (including the name and location of the relevant sub-processor and the activities it will perform) by sending an email to the Notification Email Address. Administrator may object to any new Sub-processor by notifying Alaio promptly in writing within five (5) business days after receipt of Alaio notice. In the event Administrator objects to a new Sub-processor, Alaio will use reasonable efforts to make available to Administrator a change in the Services or recommend a commercially reasonable change to Administrator´s configuration or use of the Services to avoid Processing of Personal Data by the objected-to new Sub-processor without unreasonably burdening the User. If Alaio is unable to make available such change within a reasonable period of time, which shall not exceed thirty (30) days, Administrator may terminate the applicable Order with respect only to those Services which cannot be provided by Alaio without the use of the objected-to new Sub-processor by providing written notice to Alaio. Alaio will refund Administrator any prepaid fees covering the remainder of the term of such Order following the effective date of termination with respect to such terminated Services, without imposing a pena

seed S137 document.appendix_1.appendix_2.clause_9

9. SUBPROCESSOR SECURITY
Before onboarding Sub-processors, Alaio conducts an audit of the security and privacy practices of Sub-processors to ensure Sub-processors provide a level of security and privacy appropriate to their access to data and the scope of the services they are engaged to provide. Once Alaio has assessed the risks presented by the Sub-processor then, subject always to the requirements set out in Section 7 the Sub-processor is required to enter into appropriate security, confidentiality and privacy contract terms.

seed S31 document.subclause_5.1
spanId=doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4::section::11243-11859 · chars=[11243, 11859) · scope=unspecified · doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4 · document.subclause_5.1
5.1 Account deletion. Paid plans devolve to the free plan according to the process described above. If the Bitrix24 Customer Account is on a free plan (either converted to a free plan or originally on a free plan) and is completely inactive over the course of 30 days, it is 'archived', and it can be retrieved only by an administrator account (yourself or a user in the instance with administrator rights). To retrieve the account, a User without administrative rights simply needs to log in.
I
f no User logs in for another 15 days after the instance has been 'archived', Bitrix24 Customer Account will be deleted.


seed S32 document.subclause_2.5.2
spanId=doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4::section::11860-12426 · chars=[11860, 12426) · scope=unspecified · doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4 · document.subclause_2.5.2
2.5.2 The Administrator may initiate deletion of the Bitrix24 Customer Account by first dismissing other Administrators, if any, which will entail deletion of all the connected User Accounts and all connected User Content, subject to the availability of such feature. In this case, Bitrix24 Customer Account will be made inactive immediately and completely deleted over the course of 90 (ninety) calendar days. However, this operation will not delete completely User´s Bitrix24 network accounts which will remain active for use in another Bitrix24 Customer Accounts.

4 Objection Right for New Sub-processors.
When any new Sub-processor is engaged during the Term, and if the GDPR applies to the processing of Personal Data, Alaio will, at least 10 days before the new Sub-processor processes any Personal Data, inform Administrator of the engagement (including the name and location of the relevant sub-processor and the activities it will perform) by sending an email to the Notification Email Address. Administrator may object to any new Sub-processor by notifying Alaio promptly in writing within five (5) business days after receipt of Alaio notice. In the event Administrator objects to a new Sub-processor, Alaio will use reasonable efforts to make available to Administrator a change in the Services or recommend a commercially reasonable change to Administrator´s configuration or use of the Services to avoid Processing of Personal Data by the objected-to new Sub-processor without unreasonably burdening the User. If Alaio is unable to make available such change within a reasonable period of time, which shall not exceed thirty (30) days, Administrator may terminate the applicable Order with respect only to those Services which cannot be provided by Alaio without the use of the objected-to new Sub-processor by providing written notice to Alaio. Alaio will refund Administrator any prepaid fees covering the remainder of the term of such Order following the effective date of termination with respect to such terminated Services, without imposing a pena

DATA PROCESSING AGREEMENT
Last updated 29 th  November 2024
This Data Processing Agreement ('DPA', 'Agreement') is an addendum to and forms part of the master agreement between the Administrator of Bitrix24 Customer Account and Alaio (https://www.bitrix24.com/terms/) to reflect the parties' agreement for the provision of the Processor Services (as amended from time to time) and processing of Bitrix24 Customer Account User's Personal Data in accordance with the requirements of the Data Protection Legislation.
This Data Processing Agreement will be effective from the Effective Date.
BY CLICKING THE "I ACCEPT" BUTTON BELOW, YOU (A) ACKNOWLEDGE THAT YOU HAVE READ AND UNDERSTAND THIS AGREEMENT; (B) REPRESENT AND WARRANT THAT YOU HAVE THE RIGHT, POWER, AND AUTHORITY TO ENTER INTO THIS AGREEMENT; AND (C) ACCEPT THIS AGREEMENT AND AGREE THAT YOU ARE LEGALLY BOUND BY ITS TERMS.

9. SUBPROCESSOR SECURITY
Before onboarding Sub-processors, Alaio conducts an audit of the security and privacy practices of Sub-processors to ensure Sub-processors provide a level of security and privacy appropriate to their access to data and the scope of the services they are engaged to provide. Once Alaio has assessed the risks presented by the Sub-processor then, subject always to the requirements set out in Section 7 the Sub-processor is required to enter into appropriate security, confidentiality and privacy contract terms.

'Data Incident' means a breach of Bitrix24 security leading to the accidental or unlawful destruction, loss, alteration, unauthorised disclosure of, or access to, User Personal Data on systems managed by or otherwise controlled by Alaio . 'Data Incidents' will not include unsuccessful attempts or activities that do not compromise the security of User Personal Data, including unsuccessful log-in attempts, pings, port scans, denial of service attacks, and other network attacks on firewalls or networked systems.

seed S132 document.appendix_1.appendix_2.section_business_continuity_plan
spanId=doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4::section::41182-41647 · chars=[41182, 41647) · scope=unspecified · doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4 · document.appendix_1.appendix_2.section_business_continuity_plan
BUSINESS CONTINUITY PLAN
The AWS Business Continuity Plan outlines measures to avoid and lessen environmental disruptions. It includes operational details about steps to take before, during, and after an event. The Business Continuity Plan is supported by testing that includes simulations of different scenarios. During and after testing, AWS documents people and process performance, corrective actions, and lessons learned with the aim of continuous improvement.


seed S135 document.appendix_1.appendix_2.section_governance_risk
spanId=doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4::section::42887-43935 · chars=[42887, 43935) · scope=unspecified · doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4 · document.appendix_1.appendix_2.section_governance_risk
GOVERNANCE & RISK
Data Center risk management. The AWS Security Operations Center performs regular threat and vulnerability reviews of data centers. Ongoing assessment and mitigation of potential vulnerabilities is performed through data center risk assessment activities. This assessment is performed in addition to the enterprise-level risk assessment process used to identify and manage risks presented to the business as a whole. This process also takes regional regulatory and environmental risks into consideration.
Third-party security attestation. Third-party testing of AWS data centers, as documented in our third-party reports, ensures AWS has appropriately implemented security measures aligned to established rules needed to obtain security certifications. Depending on the compliance program and its requirements, external auditors may perform testing of media disposal, review security camera footage, observe entrances and hallways throughout a data center, test electronic access control devices, and examine data center equipment.

3.2 Specific for data transfers from EU, UK and Switzerland to the USA, Alaio, Inc complies with the EU-U.S. Data Privacy Framework (EU-U.S. DPF), the UK Extension to the EU-U.S. DPF, and the Swiss-U.S. Data Privacy Framework (SwissU.S. DPF) as set forth by the U.S. Department of Commerce.  Alaio, Inc has certified to the U.S. Department of Commerce that it adheres to the EU-U.S. Data Privacy Framework Principles (EU-U.S. DPF Principles) with regard to the processing of personal data received from the European Union in reliance on the EU-U.S. DPF and from the United Kingdom (and Gibraltar) in reliance on the UK Extension to the EU-U.S. DPF.  Alaio, Inc has certified to the U.S. Department of Commerce that it adheres to the Swiss-U.S. Data Privacy Framework Principles (Swiss-U.S. DPF Principles) with regard to the processing of personal data received from Switzerland in reliance on the Swiss-U.S. DPF.  If there is any conflict between the terms in this DPA and the EU-U.S. DPF Principles and/or the Swiss-U.S. DPF Principles, the Principles shall govern.  To learn more about the Data Privacy Framework (DPF) program, and to view our certification, please
visit https://www.dataprivacyframework.gov/. The Data Privacy Framework (DPF) list of participating organisations is available at https://www.dataprivacyframework.gov/s/participant-search. Under certain conditions, you can invoke the corresponding binding arbitration.
The Federal Trade Commission has jurisdiction over Alaio, Inc

DATA PROCESSING AGREEMENT
Last updated 29 th  November 2024
This Data Processing Agreement ('DPA', 'Agreement') is an addendum to and forms part of the master agreement between the Administrator of Bitrix24 Customer Account and Alaio (https://www.bitrix24.com/terms/) to reflect the parties' agreement for the provision of the Processor Services (as amended from time to time) and processing of Bitrix24 Customer Account User's Personal Data in accordance with the requirements of the Data Protection Legislation.
This Data Processing Agreement will be effective from the Effective Date.
BY CLICKING THE "I ACCEPT" BUTTON BELOW, YOU (A) ACKNOWLEDGE THAT YOU HAVE READ AND UNDERSTAND THIS AGREEMENT; (B) REPRESENT AND WARRANT THAT YOU HAVE THE RIGHT, POWER, AND AUTHORITY TO ENTER INTO THIS AGREEMENT; AND (C) ACCEPT THIS AGREEMENT AND AGREE THAT YOU ARE LEGALLY BOUND BY ITS TERMS.

4 Objection Right for New Sub-processors.
When any new Sub-processor is engaged during the Term, and if the GDPR applies to the processing of Personal Data, Alaio will, at least 10 days before the new Sub-processor processes any Personal Data, inform Administrator of the engagement (including the name and location of the relevant sub-processor and the activities it will perform) by sending an email to the Notification Email Address. Administrator may object to any new Sub-processor by notifying Alaio promptly in writing within five (5) business days after receipt of Alaio notice. In the event Administrator objects to a new Sub-processor, Alaio will use reasonable efforts to make available to Administrator a change in the Services or recommend a commercially reasonable change to Administrator´s configuration or use of the Services to avoid Processing of Personal Data by the objected-to new Sub-processor without unreasonably burdening the User. If Alaio is unable to make available such change within a reasonable period of time, which shall not exceed thirty (30) days, Administrator may terminate the applicable Order with respect only to those Services which cannot be provided by Alaio without the use of the objected-to new Sub-processor by providing written notice to Alaio. Alaio will refund Administrator any prepaid fees covering the remainder of the term of such Order following the effective date of termination with respect to such terminated Services, without imposing a pena

seed S135 document.appendix_1.appendix_2.section_governance_risk
spanId=doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4::section::42887-43935 · chars=[42887, 43935) · scope=unspecified · doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4 · document.appendix_1.appendix_2.section_governance_risk
GOVERNANCE & RISK
Data Center risk management. The AWS Security Operations Center performs regular threat and vulnerability reviews of data centers. Ongoing assessment and mitigation of potential vulnerabilities is performed through data center risk assessment activities. This assessment is performed in addition to the enterprise-level risk assessment process used to identify and manage risks presented to the business as a whole. This process also takes regional regulatory and environmental risks into consideration.
Third-party security attestation. Third-party testing of AWS data centers, as documented in our third-party reports, ensures AWS has appropriately implemented security measures aligned to established rules needed to obtain security certifications. Depending on the compliance program and its requirements, external auditors may perform testing of media disposal, review security camera footage, observe entrances and hallways throughout a data center, test electronic access control devices, and examine data center equipment.

DATA PROCESSING AGREEMENT
Last updated 29 th  November 2024
This Data Processing Agreement ('DPA', 'Agreement') is an addendum to and forms part of the master agreement between the Administrator of Bitrix24 Customer Account and Alaio (https://www.bitrix24.com/terms/) to reflect the parties' agreement for the provision of the Processor Services (as amended from time to time) and processing of Bitrix24 Customer Account User's Personal Data in accordance with the requirements of the Data Protection Legislation.
This Data Processing Agreement will be effective from the Effective Date.

APPLICATION OF THIS DPA
If there is any conflict or inconsistency between the terms of this DPA and the Terms of Service (https://www.bitrix24.eu/terms/), the DPA will govern. Subject to the amendments in this DPA, the Terms of Service remain in full force and effect.

4 Objection Right for New Sub-processors.
When any new Sub-processor is engaged during the Term, and if the GDPR applies to the processing of Personal Data, Alaio will, at least 10 days before the new Sub-processor processes any Personal Data, inform Administrator of the engagement (including the name and location of the relevant sub-processor and the activities it will perform) by sending an email to the Notification Email Address. Administrator may object to any new Sub-processor by notifying Alaio promptly in writing within five (5) business days after receipt of Alaio notice. In the event Administrator objects to a new Sub-processor, Alaio will use reasonable efforts to make available to Administrator a change in the Services or recommend a commercially reasonable change to Administrator´s configuration or use of the Services to avoid Processing of Personal Data by the objected-to new Sub-processor without unreasonably burdening the User. If Alaio is unable to make available such change within a reasonable period of time, which shall not exceed thirty (30) days, Administrator may terminate the applicable Order with respect only to those Services which cannot be provided by Alaio without the use of the objected-to new Sub-processor by providing written notice to Alaio. Alaio will refund Administrator any prepaid fees covering the remainder of the term of such Order following the effective date of termination with respect to such terminated Services, without imposing a pena

DATA PROCESSING AGREEMENT
Last updated 29 th  November 2024
This Data Processing Agreement ('DPA', 'Agreement') is an addendum to and forms part of the master agreement between the Administrator of Bitrix24 Customer Account and Alaio (https://www.bitrix24.com/terms/) to reflect the parties' agreement for the provision of the Processor Services (as amended from time to time) and processing of Bitrix24 Customer Account User's Personal Data in accordance with the requirements of the Data Protection Legislation.
This Data Processing Agreement will be effective from the Effective Date.
BY CLICKING THE "I ACCEPT" BUTTON BELOW, YOU (A) ACKNOWLEDGE THAT YOU HAVE READ AND UNDERSTAND THIS AGREEMENT; (B) REPRESENT AND WARRANT THAT YOU HAVE THE RIGHT, POWER, AND AUTHORITY TO ENTER INTO THIS AGREEMENT; AND (C) ACCEPT THIS AGREEMENT AND AGREE THAT YOU ARE LEGALLY BOUND BY ITS TERMS.

Alaio, Inc has certified to the U.S. Department of Commerce that it adheres to the Swiss-U.S. Data Privacy Framework Principles (Swiss-U.S. DPF Principles) with regard to the processing of personal data received from Switzerland in reliance on the Swiss-U.S. DPF. Alternative Dispute Resolution Body
In compliance with the EU-U.S. DPF, the UK Extension to the EU-U.S. DPF and the SwissU.S. DPF, Alaio, Inc commits to refer unresolved complaints concerning our handling of personal data received in reliance on the EU-U.S. DPF, the UK Extension to the EU-U.S. DPF and the Swiss-U.S. DPF to JAMS, an alternative dispute resolution provider based in the United States. The services of JAMS are provided at no cost to you.

4 Objection Right for New Sub-processors.
When any new Sub-processor is engaged during the Term, and if the GDPR applies to the processing of Personal Data, Alaio will, at least 10 days before the new Sub-processor processes any Personal Data, inform Administrator of the engagement (including the name and location of the relevant sub-processor and the activities it will perform) by sending an email to the Notification Email Address. Administrator may object to any new Sub-processor by notifying Alaio promptly in writing within five (5) business days after receipt of Alaio notice. In the event Administrator objects to a new Sub-processor, Alaio will use reasonable efforts to make available to Administrator a change in the Services or recommend a commercially reasonable change to Administrator´s configuration or use of the Services to avoid Processing of Personal Data by the objected-to new Sub-processor without unreasonably burdening the User. If Alaio is unable to make available such change within a reasonable period of time, which shall not exceed thirty (30) days, Administrator may terminate the applicable Order with respect only to those Services which cannot be provided by Alaio without the use of the objected-to new Sub-processor by providing written notice to Alaio. spanId=doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4::section::2640-5596 · chars=[2640, 5596) · scope=unspecified · doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4 · document.clause_1.subclause_1.1.list_item_19
'User Personal Data' means personal data that is processed by Alaio on behalf of the Administrator as part of Alaio provision of the Processor Services.
'Data Incident' means a breach of Bitrix24 security leading to the accidental or unlawful destruction, loss, alteration, unauthorised disclosure of, or access to, User Personal Data on systems managed by or otherwise controlled by Alaio . 'Data Incidents' will not include unsuccessful attempts or activities that do not compromise the security of User Personal Data, including unsuccessful log-in attempts, pings, port scans, denial of service attacks, and other network attacks on firewalls or networked systems.
'Data Protection Legislation' means all legislation and regulatory requirements in force from time to time relating to the use of personal data and the privacy of electronic communicationsincluding, but not limited to: EU 2016/679 General Data Protection Regulation (GDPR);California Privacy Rights Act of 2020 and any other USA applicable federal and state privacy legislation; General Data Protection Law (Law No. 13.709/2018 of Brazil, (Lei Geral de Proteção de Dados); Law No. 30 of 2018 on the Protection of Personal Data of Bahrein; the federal Privacy Act 1988 of Australia; Personal Information Protection and Electronic Documents Act of Canada; The Personal Information Protection Law of China; Personal Data (Privacy) Ordinance (Cap. 486) of Hong Kong; Information Technology Act 2000 as amended by the Information Technol


seed S132 document.appendix_1.appendix_2.section_business_continuity_plan
spanId=doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4::section::41182-41647 · chars=[41182, 41647) · scope=unspecified · doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4 · document.appendix_1.appendix_2.section_business_continuity_plan
BUSINESS CONTINUITY PLAN
The AWS Business Continuity Plan outlines measures to avoid and lessen environmental disruptions. It includes operational details about steps to take before, during, and after an event. The Business Continuity Plan is supported by testing that includes simulations of different scenarios. During and after testing, AWS documents people and process performance, corrective actions, and lessons learned with the aim of continuous improvement.

Alternative Dispute Resolution Body
In compliance with the EU-U.S. DPF, the UK Extension to the EU-U.S. DPF and the SwissU.S. DPF, Alaio, Inc commits to refer unresolved complaints concerning our handling of personal data received in reliance on the EU-U.S. DPF, the UK Extension to the EU-U.S. DPF and the Swiss-U.S. DPF to JAMS, an alternative dispute resolution provider based in the United States. DATA PROCESSING AGREEMENT
Last updated 29 th  November 2024
This Data Processing Agreement ('DPA', 'Agreement') is an addendum to and forms part of the master agreement between the Administrator of Bitrix24 Customer Account and Alaio (https://www.bitrix24.com/terms/) to reflect the parties' agreement for the provision of the Processor Services (as amended from time to time) and processing of Bitrix24 Customer Account User's Personal Data in accordance with the requirements of the Data Protection Legislation.
This Data Processing Agreement will be effective from the Effective Date.
BY CLICKING THE "I ACCEPT" BUTTON BELOW, YOU (A) ACKNOWLEDGE THAT YOU HAVE READ AND UNDERSTAND THIS AGREEMENT; (B) REPRESENT AND WARRANT THAT YOU HAVE THE RIGHT, POWER, AND AUTHORITY TO ENTER INTO THIS AGREEMENT; AND (C) ACCEPT THIS AGREEMENT AND AGREE THAT YOU ARE LEGALLY BOUND BY ITS TERMS.

2 Alaio Security Measures. Alaio will implement and maintain technical, physical and organisational measures to protect confidentiality and integrity of Personal Data against accidental or unlawful destruction, loss, alteration, unauthorised disclosure or access as described in the Appendix 2 to this DPA (the 'Security Measures'). As described in the Appendix 2 to this DPA, the Security Measures include measures: (a) to help ensure the ongoing confidentiality, integrity, availability and resilience of Bitrix24 systems and services;


seed S135 document.appendix_1.appendix_2.section_governance_risk
spanId=doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4::section::42887-43935 · chars=[42887, 43935) · scope=unspecified · doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4 · document.appendix_1.appendix_2.section_governance_risk
GOVERNANCE & RISK
Data Center risk management. The AWS Security Operations Center performs regular threat and vulnerability reviews of data centers. Ongoing assessment and mitigation of potential vulnerabilities is performed through data center risk assessment activities. This assessment is performed in addition to the enterprise-level risk assessment process used to identify and manage risks presented to the business as a whole. This process also takes regional regulatory and environmental risks into consideration.
Third-party security attestation. Third-party testing of AWS data centers, as documented in our third-party reports, ensures AWS has appropriately implemented security measures aligned to established rules needed to obtain security certifications. Depending on the compliance program and its requirements, external auditors may perform testing of media disposal, review security camera footage, observe entrances and hallways throughout a data center, test electronic access control devices, and examine data center equipment.

Appendix 2 SECURITY MEASURES
Alaio will implement and maintain the Security Measures set out in this Appendix 2. Alaio may update or modify such Security Measures from time to time, provided that such updates and modifications do not result in the degradation of the overall security of the Processor Services.

seed S137 document.appendix_1.appendix_2.clause_9
spanId=doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4::section::44737-45272 · chars=[44737, 45272) · scope=unspecified · doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4 · document.appendix_1.appendix_2.clause_9
9. SUBPROCESSOR SECURITY
Before onboarding Sub-processors, Alaio conducts an audit of the security and privacy practices of Sub-processors to ensure Sub-processors provide a level of security and privacy appropriate to their access to data and the scope of the services they are engaged to provide. Once Alaio has assessed the risks presented by the Sub-processor then, subject always to the requirements set out in Section 7 the Sub-processor is required to enter into appropriate security, confidentiality and privacy contract terms.

seed S136 document.appendix_1.appendix_2.list_item_(b)
spanId=doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4::section::43936-44736 · chars=[43936, 44736) · scope=unspecified · doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4 · document.appendix_1.appendix_2.list_item_(b)
(b) Networks & Transmission .
Data Transmission . Alaio Datacenters are connected via private links protected by AWS Network firewalls to provide secure data transfer. This is designed to protect the confidentiality, integrity and availability of the network and prevent data from being read, copied, altered or removed without authorization during electronic transfer.
Data Breach Response . Alaio monitors a variety of communication channels for security breaches, and Alaio  security personnel will react promptly to known incidents.
External Attack Surface . Alaio considers potential attack vectors and incorporates appropriate purpose built proprietary technologies into external facing systems.
Encryption Technologies . Alaio uses HTTPS encryption (also referred to as SSL or TLS connection).


seed S10 document.clause_1.subclause_1.1.list_item_19
spanId=doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4::section::2640-5596 · chars=[2640, 5596) · scope=unspecified · doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4 · document.clause_1.subclause_1.1.list_item_19
'User Personal Data' means personal data that is processed by Alaio on behalf of the Administrator as part of Alaio provision of the Processor Services.
'Data Incident' means a breach of Bitrix24 security leading to the accidental or unlawful destruction, loss, alteration, unauthorised disclosure of, or access to, User Personal Data on systems managed by or otherwise controlled by Alaio . 'Data Incidents' will not include unsuccessful attempts or activities that do not compromise the security of User Personal Data, including unsuccessful log-in attempts, pings, port scans, denial of service attacks, and other network attacks on firewalls or networked systems.
'Data Protection Legislation' means all legislation and regulatory requirements in force from time to time relating to the use of personal data and the privacy of electronic communicationsincluding, but not limited to: EU 2016/679 General Data Protection Regulation (GDPR);California Privacy Rights Act of 2020 and any other USA applicable federal and state privacy legislation; General Data Protection Law (Law No. 13.709/2018 of Brazil, (Lei Geral de Proteção de Dados); Law No. 30 of 2018 on the Protection of Personal Data of Bahrein; the federal Privacy Act 1988 of Australia; Personal Information Protection and Electronic Documents Act of Canada; The Personal Information Protection Law of China; Personal Data (Privacy) Ordinance (Cap. 486) of Hong Kong; Information Technology Act 2000 as amended by the Information Technol

11.3.3 Disputes resolution
Alaio, Inc Internal Complaints MechanismIn compliance with the EU-U.S. DPF, the UK Extension to the EU-U.S. DPF and the Swiss-U.S. DPF, Alaio, Inc commits to resolve DPF Principles-related complaints about our collection and use of your personal information. EU, UK and Swiss individuals with inquiries or complaints regarding our handling of personal data received in reliance on the EU-U.S. DPF, the UK Extension to the EU-U.S. DPF and the Swiss-U.S. DPF should first contact Alaio, Inc at: privacy@bitrix24.com


seed S65 document.section_alternative_dispute_resolution_body
spanId=doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4::section::26252-26970 · chars=[26252, 26970) · scope=unspecified · doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4 · document.section_alternative_dispute_resolution_body
Alternative Dispute Resolution Body
In compliance with the EU-U.S. DPF, the UK Extension to the EU-U.S. DPF and the SwissU.S. DPF, Alaio, Inc commits to refer unresolved complaints concerning our handling of personal data received in reliance on the EU-U.S. DPF, the UK Extension to the EU-U.S. DPF and the Swiss-U.S. DPF to JAMS, an alternative dispute resolution provider based in the United States. If you do not receive timely acknowledgment of your DPF Principlesrelated complaint from us, or if we have not addressed your DPF Principles-related complaint to your satisfaction, please visit https://www.jamsadr.com/for more information or to file a complaint.  The services of JAMS are provided at no cost to you.

DATA PROCESSING AGREEMENT
Last updated 29 th  November 2024
This Data Processing Agreement ('DPA', 'Agreement') is an addendum to and forms part of the master agreement between the Administrator of Bitrix24 Customer Account and Alaio (https://www.bitrix24.com/terms/) to reflect the parties' agreement for the provision of the Processor Services (as amended from time to time) and processing of Bitrix24 Customer Account User's Personal Data in accordance with the requirements of the Data Protection Legislation.
This Data Processing Agreement will be effective from the Effective Date.
BY CLICKING THE "I ACCEPT" BUTTON BELOW, YOU (A) ACKNOWLEDGE THAT YOU HAVE READ AND UNDERSTAND THIS AGREEMENT; (B) REPRESENT AND WARRANT THAT YOU HAVE THE RIGHT, POWER, AND AUTHORITY TO ENTER INTO THIS AGREEMENT; AND (C) ACCEPT THIS AGREEMENT AND AGREE THAT YOU ARE LEGALLY BOUND BY ITS TERMS.

4 Objection Right for New Sub-processors.
When any new Sub-processor is engaged during the Term, and if the GDPR applies to the processing of Personal Data, Alaio will, at least 10 days before the new Sub-processor processes any Personal Data, inform Administrator of the engagement (including the name and location of the relevant sub-processor and the activities it will perform) by sending an email to the Notification Email Address. Administrator may object to any new Sub-processor by notifying Alaio promptly in writing within five (5) business days after receipt of Alaio notice. In the event Administrator objects to a new Sub-processor, Alaio will use reasonable efforts to make available to Administrator a change in the Services or recommend a commercially reasonable change to Administrator´s configuration or use of the Services to avoid Processing of Personal Data by the objected-to new Sub-processor without unreasonably burdening the User. If Alaio is unable to make available such change within a reasonable period of time, which shall not exceed thirty (30) days, Administrator may terminate the applicable Order with respect only to those Services which cannot be provided by Alaio without the use of the objected-to new Sub-processor by providing written notice to Alaio. Alaio will refund Administrator any prepaid fees covering the remainder of the term of such Order following the effective date of termination with respect to such terminated Services, without imposing a pena


seed S136 document.appendix_1.appendix_2.list_item_(b)
spanId=doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4::section::43936-44736 · chars=[43936, 44736) · scope=unspecified · doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4 · document.appendix_1.appendix_2.list_item_(b)
(b) Networks & Transmission .
Data Transmission . Alaio Datacenters are connected via private links protected by AWS Network firewalls to provide secure data transfer. This is designed to protect the confidentiality, integrity and availability of the network and prevent data from being read, copied, altered or removed without authorization during electronic transfer.
Data Breach Response . Alaio monitors a variety of communication channels for security breaches, and Alaio  security personnel will react promptly to known incidents.
External Attack Surface . Alaio considers potential attack vectors and incorporates appropriate purpose built proprietary technologies into external facing systems.
Encryption Technologies . Alaio uses HTTPS encryption (also referred to as SSL or TLS connection).

DATA PROCESSING AGREEMENT
Last updated 29 th  November 2024
This Data Processing Agreement ('DPA', 'Agreement') is an addendum to and forms part of the master agreement between the Administrator of Bitrix24 Customer Account and Alaio (https://www.bitrix24.com/terms/) to reflect the parties' agreement for the provision of the Processor Services (as amended from time to time) and processing of Bitrix24 Customer Account User's Personal Data in accordance with the requirements of the Data Protection Legislation.
This Data Processing Agreement will be effective from the Effective Date.
BY CLICKING THE "I ACCEPT" BUTTON BELOW, YOU (A) ACKNOWLEDGE THAT YOU HAVE READ AND UNDERSTAND THIS AGREEMENT; (B) REPRESENT AND WARRANT THAT YOU HAVE THE RIGHT, POWER, AND AUTHORITY TO ENTER INTO THIS AGREEMENT; AND (C) ACCEPT THIS AGREEMENT AND AGREE THAT YOU ARE LEGALLY BOUND BY ITS TERMS.

If there is any conflict or inconsistency between the terms of this DPA and the Terms of Service (https://www.bitrix24.eu/terms/), the DPA will govern. Subject to the amendments in this DPA, the Terms of Service remain in full force and effect.

Alternative Dispute Resolution Body
In compliance with the EU-U.S. DPF, the UK Extension to the EU-U.S. DPF and the SwissU.S. DPF, Alaio, Inc commits to refer unresolved complaints concerning our handling of personal data received in reliance on the EU-U.S. DPF, the UK Extension to the EU-U.S. DPF and the Swiss-U.S. DPF to JAMS, an alternative dispute resolution provider based in the United States. 3. DURATION OF THIS DPA
This DPA will take effect on the Effective Date and, notwithstanding expiry of the Term, remain in effect until, and automatically expire upon, deletion of all User Personal Data by Bitrix24 as described in this DPA.

seed S135 document.appendix_1.appendix_2.section_governance_risk
spanId=doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4::section::42887-43935 · chars=[42887, 43935) · scope=unspecified · doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4 · document.appendix_1.appendix_2.section_governance_risk
GOVERNANCE & RISK
Data Center risk management. The AWS Security Operations Center performs regular threat and vulnerability reviews of data centers. Ongoing assessment and mitigation of potential vulnerabilities is performed through data center risk assessment activities. This assessment is performed in addition to the enterprise-level risk assessment process used to identify and manage risks presented to the business as a whole. This process also takes regional regulatory and environmental risks into consideration.
Third-party security attestation. Third-party testing of AWS data centers, as documented in our third-party reports, ensures AWS has appropriately implemented security measures aligned to established rules needed to obtain security certifications. Depending on the compliance program and its requirements, external auditors may perform testing of media disposal, review security camera footage, observe entrances and hallways throughout a data center, test electronic access control devices, and examine data center equipment.

DATA PROCESSING AGREEMENT
Last updated 29 th  November 2024
This Data Processing Agreement ('DPA', 'Agreement') is an addendum to and forms part of the master agreement between the Administrator of Bitrix24 Customer Account and Alaio (https://www.bitrix24.com/terms/) to reflect the parties' agreement for the provision of the Processor Services (as amended from time to time) and processing of Bitrix24 Customer Account User's Personal Data in accordance with the requirements of the Data Protection Legislation.
This Data Processing Agreement will be effective from the Effective Date.
BY CLICKING THE "I ACCEPT" BUTTON BELOW, YOU (A) ACKNOWLEDGE THAT YOU HAVE READ AND UNDERSTAND THIS AGREEMENT; (B) REPRESENT AND WARRANT THAT YOU HAVE THE RIGHT, POWER, AND AUTHORITY TO ENTER INTO THIS AGREEMENT; AND (C) ACCEPT THIS AGREEMENT AND AGREE THAT YOU ARE LEGALLY BOUND BY ITS TERMS.

1.  INTRODUCTION
This DPA reflect the parties' agreement on the terms governing the processing and security of User Personal Data in connection with the Data Protection Legislation.

3.2 Specific for data transfers from EU, UK and Switzerland to the USA, Alaio, Inc complies with the EU-U.S. Data Privacy Framework (EU-U.S. DPF), the UK Extension to the EU-U.S. DPF, and the Swiss-U.S. Data Privacy Framework (SwissU.S. DPF) as set forth by the U.S. Department of Commerce.  Alaio, Inc has certified to the U.S. Department of Commerce that it adheres to the EU-U.S. Data Privacy Framework Principles (EU-U.S. DPF Principles) with regard to the processing of personal data received from the European Union in reliance on the EU-U.S. DPF and from the United Kingdom (and Gibraltar) in reliance on the UK Extension to the EU-U.S. DPF.  Alaio, Inc has certified to the U.S. Department of Commerce that it adheres to the Swiss-U.S. Data Privacy Framework Principles (Swiss-U.S. DPF Principles) with regard to the processing of personal data received from Switzerland in reliance on the Swiss-U.S. DPF.  If there is any conflict between the terms in this DPA and the EU-U.S. DPF Principles and/or the Swiss-U.S. DPF Principles, the Principles shall govern.  To learn more about the Data Privacy Framework (DPF) program, and to view our certification, please
visit https://www.dataprivacyframework.gov/. The Data Privacy Framework (DPF) list of participating organisations is available at https://www.dataprivacyframework.gov/s/participant-search. Under certain conditions, you can invoke the corresponding binding arbitration.

spanId=doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4::section::2640-5596 · chars=[2640, 5596) · scope=unspecified · doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4 · document.clause_1.subclause_1.1.list_item_19
'User Personal Data' means personal data that is processed by Alaio on behalf of the Administrator as part of Alaio provision of the Processor Services.
'Data Incident' means a breach of Bitrix24 security leading to the accidental or unlawful destruction, loss, alteration, unauthorised disclosure of, or access to, User Personal Data on systems managed by or otherwise controlled by Alaio . 'Data Incidents' will not include unsuccessful attempts or activities that do not compromise the security of User Personal Data, including unsuccessful log-in attempts, pings, port scans, denial of service attacks, and other network attacks on firewalls or networked systems.
'Data Protection Legislation' means all legislation and regulatory requirements in force from time to time relating to the use of personal data and the privacy of electronic communicationsincluding, but not limited to: EU 2016/679 General Data Protection Regulation (GDPR);California Privacy Rights Act of 2020 and any other USA applicable federal and state privacy legislation; General Data Protection Law (Law No. 13.709/2018 of Brazil, (Lei Geral de Proteção de Dados); Law No. 30 of 2018 on the Protection of Personal Data of Bahrein; the federal Privacy Act 1988 of Australia; Personal Information Protection and Electronic Documents Act of Canada; The Personal Information Protection Law of China; Personal Data (Privacy) Ordinance (Cap. 486) of Hong Kong; Information Technology Act 2000 as amended by the Information Technol

seed S65 document.section_alternative_dispute_resolution_body
spanId=doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4::section::26252-26970 · chars=[26252, 26970) · scope=unspecified · doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4 · document.section_alternative_dispute_resolution_body
Alternative Dispute Resolution Body
In compliance with the EU-U.S. DPF, the UK Extension to the EU-U.S. DPF and the SwissU.S. DPF, Alaio, Inc commits to refer unresolved complaints concerning our handling of personal data received in reliance on the EU-U.S. DPF, the UK Extension to the EU-U.S. DPF and the Swiss-U.S. DPF to JAMS, an alternative dispute resolution provider based in the United States. If you do not receive timely acknowledgment of your DPF Principlesrelated complaint from us, or if we have not addressed your DPF Principles-related complaint to your satisfaction, please visit https://www.jamsadr.com/for more information or to file a complaint.  The services of JAMS are provided at no cost to you.

5 Deletion on Term Expiry. On expiry of the Term, Administrator instructs Alaio to delete all Personal Data (including existing copies) from Bitrix24 systems in accordance with applicable law. Alaio will comply with this instruction as soon as reasonably practicable and within a maximum period of 90 days, unless any applicable law requires storage.


seed S31 document.subclause_5.1
spanId=doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4::section::11243-11859 · chars=[11243, 11859) · scope=unspecified · doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4 · document.subclause_5.1
5.1 Account deletion. Paid plans devolve to the free plan according to the process described above. If the Bitrix24 Customer Account is on a free plan (either converted to a free plan or originally on a free plan) and is completely inactive over the course of 30 days, it is 'archived', and it can be retrieved only by an administrator account (yourself or a user in the instance with administrator rights). To retrieve the account, a User without administrative rights simply needs to log in.
I
f no User logs in for another 15 days after the instance has been 'archived', Bitrix24 Customer Account will be deleted.

In this case, Bitrix24 Customer Account will be made inactive immediately and completely deleted over the course of 90 (ninety) calendar days. 3. DURATION OF THIS DPA
This DPA will take effect on the Effective Date and, notwithstanding expiry of the Term, remain in effect until, and automatically expire upon, deletion of all User Personal Data by Bitrix24 as described in this DPA.

seed S59 document.clause_10

10. DATA PROTECTION IMPACT ASSESSMENT
Upon Administrator´s  request, Alaio will assist Administrator in ensuring compliance with any obligations of Administrator in respect of data protection impact assessments and prior consultation, including (if applicable) Administrator´s  obligations pursuant to Articles 35 and 36 of the GDPR, to the extent User does not otherwise have access to the relevant information, and to the extent such information is available to Alaio. Alaio shall provide reasonable assistance to Administrator in the cooperation or prior consultation with the Supervisory Authority in the performance of its tasks.

(b) Networks & Transmission .
Data Transmission . Alaio Datacenters are connected via private links protected by AWS Network firewalls to provide secure data transfer. External Attack Surface . Alaio considers potential attack vectors and incorporates appropriate purpose built proprietary technologies into external facing systems.
Encryption Technologies . Alaio uses HTTPS encryption (also referred to as SSL or TLS connection).

DATA PROCESSING AGREEMENT
Last updated 29 th  November 2024
This Data Processing Agreement ('DPA', 'Agreement') is an addendum to and forms part of the master agreement between the Administrator of Bitrix24 Customer Account and Alaio (https://www.bitrix24.com/terms/) to reflect the parties' agreement for the provision of the Processor Services (as amended from time to time) and processing of Bitrix24 Customer Account User's Personal Data in accordance with the requirements of the Data Protection Legislation.
This Data Processing Agreement will be effective from the Effective Date.
BY CLICKING THE "I ACCEPT" BUTTON BELOW, YOU (A) ACKNOWLEDGE THAT YOU HAVE READ AND UNDERSTAND THIS AGREEMENT; (B) REPRESENT AND WARRANT THAT YOU HAVE THE RIGHT, POWER, AND AUTHORITY TO ENTER INTO THIS AGREEMENT; AND (C) ACCEPT THIS AGREEMENT AND AGREE THAT YOU ARE LEGALLY BOUND BY ITS TERMS.

European Commission decided that standard contractual clauses offer sufficient safeguards on data protection for the data to be transferred internationally. You have a right to request information on those contractual safeguards (Please contact our Data Protection Officer).

4 Objection Right for New Sub-processors.
When any new Sub-processor is engaged during the Term, and if the GDPR applies to the processing of Personal Data, Alaio will, at least 10 days before the new Sub-processor processes any Personal Data, inform Administrator of the engagement (including the name and location of the relevant sub-processor and the activities it will perform) by sending an email to the Notification Email Address. Administrator may object to any new Sub-processor by notifying Alaio promptly in writing within five (5) business days after receipt of Alaio notice. In the event Administrator objects to a new Sub-processor, Alaio will use reasonable efforts to make available to Administrator a change in the Services or recommend a commercially reasonable change to Administrator´s configuration or use of the Services to avoid Processing of Personal Data by the objected-to new Sub-processor without unreasonably burdening the User. If Alaio is unable to make available such change within a reasonable period of time, which shall not exceed thirty (30) days, Administrator may terminate the applicable Order with respect only to those Services which cannot be provided by Alaio without the use of the objected-to new Sub-processor by providing written notice to Alaio. Alaio will refund Administrator any prepaid fees covering the remainder of the term of such Order following the effective date of termination with respect to such terminated Services, without imposing a pena

seed S65 document.section_alternative_dispute_resolution_body
spanId=doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4::section::26252-26970 · chars=[26252, 26970) · scope=unspecified · doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4 · document.section_alternative_dispute_resolution_body
Alternative Dispute Resolution Body
In compliance with the EU-U.S. DPF, the UK Extension to the EU-U.S. DPF and the SwissU.S. DPF, Alaio, Inc commits to refer unresolved complaints concerning our handling of personal data received in reliance on the EU-U.S. DPF, the UK Extension to the EU-U.S. DPF and the Swiss-U.S. DPF to JAMS, an alternative dispute resolution provider based in the United States. If you do not receive timely acknowledgment of your DPF Principlesrelated complaint from us, or if we have not addressed your DPF Principles-related complaint to your satisfaction, please visit https://www.jamsadr.com/for more information or to file a complaint.  The services of JAMS are provided at no cost to you.


seed S31 document.subclause_5.1
spanId=doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4::section::11243-11859 · chars=[11243, 11859) · scope=unspecified · doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4 · document.subclause_5.1
5.1 Account deletion. Paid plans devolve to the free plan according to the process described above. If the Bitrix24 Customer Account is on a free plan (either converted to a free plan or originally on a free plan) and is completely inactive over the course of 30 days, it is 'archived', and it can be retrieved only by an administrator account (yourself or a user in the instance with administrator rights). To retrieve the account, a User without administrative rights simply needs to log in.
I
f no User logs in for another 15 days after the instance has been 'archived', Bitrix24 Customer Account will be deleted.

4 Objection Right for New Sub-processors.
When any new Sub-processor is engaged during the Term, and if the GDPR applies to the processing of Personal Data, Alaio will, at least 10 days before the new Sub-processor processes any Personal Data, inform Administrator of the engagement (including the name and location of the relevant sub-processor and the activities it will perform) by sending an email to the Notification Email Address. Administrator may object to any new Sub-processor by notifying Alaio promptly in writing within five (5) business days after receipt of Alaio notice. In the event Administrator objects to a new Sub-processor, Alaio will use reasonable efforts to make available to Administrator a change in the Services or recommend a commercially reasonable change to Administrator´s configuration or use of the Services to avoid Processing of Personal Data by the objected-to new Sub-processor without unreasonably burdening the User. If Alaio is unable to make available such change within a reasonable period of time, which shall not exceed thirty (30) days, Administrator may terminate the applicable Order with respect only to those Services which cannot be provided by Alaio without the use of the objected-to new Sub-processor by providing written notice to Alaio. Alaio will refund Administrator any prepaid fees covering the remainder of the term of such Order following the effective date of termination with respect to such terminated Services, without imposing a pena

DATA PROCESSING AGREEMENT
Last updated 29 th  November 2024
This Data Processing Agreement ('DPA', 'Agreement') is an addendum to and forms part of the master agreement between the Administrator of Bitrix24 Customer Account and Alaio (https://www.bitrix24.com/terms/) to reflect the parties' agreement for the provision of the Processor Services (as amended from time to time) and processing of Bitrix24 Customer Account User's Personal Data in accordance with the requirements of the Data Protection Legislation.
This Data Processing Agreement will be effective from the Effective Date.
BY CLICKING THE "I ACCEPT" BUTTON BELOW, YOU (A) ACKNOWLEDGE THAT YOU HAVE READ AND UNDERSTAND THIS AGREEMENT; (B) REPRESENT AND WARRANT THAT YOU HAVE THE RIGHT, POWER, AND AUTHORITY TO ENTER INTO THIS AGREEMENT; AND (C) ACCEPT THIS AGREEMENT AND AGREE THAT YOU ARE LEGALLY BOUND BY ITS TERMS.

seed S65 document.section_alternative_dispute_resolution_body
spanId=doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4::section::26252-26970 · chars=[26252, 26970) · scope=unspecified · doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4 · document.section_alternative_dispute_resolution_body
Alternative Dispute Resolution Body
In compliance with the EU-U.S. DPF, the UK Extension to the EU-U.S. DPF and the SwissU.S. DPF, Alaio, Inc commits to refer unresolved complaints concerning our handling of personal data received in reliance on the EU-U.S. DPF, the UK Extension to the EU-U.S. DPF and the Swiss-U.S. DPF to JAMS, an alternative dispute resolution provider based in the United States. If you do not receive timely acknowledgment of your DPF Principlesrelated complaint from us, or if we have not addressed your DPF Principles-related complaint to your satisfaction, please visit https://www.jamsadr.com/for more information or to file a complaint.  The services of JAMS are provided at no cost to you.

seed S134 document.appendix_1.appendix_2.section_nfrastructure_maintenance
spanId=doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4::section::42172-42886 · chars=[42172, 42886) · scope=unspecified · doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4 · document.appendix_1.appendix_2.section_nfrastructure_maintenance
NFRASTRUCTURE MAINTENANCE
Equipment maintenance. AWS monitors and performs preventative maintenance of electrical and mechanical equipment to maintain the continued operability of systems within AWS data centers. Equipment maintenance procedures are carried out by qualified persons and completed according to a documented maintenance schedule.
Environment management . AWS monitors electrical and mechanical systems and equipment to enable immediate identification of issues. This is carried out by utilizing continuous audit tools and information provided through our Building Management and Electrical Monitoring Systems. Preventative maintenance is performed to maintain the continued operability of equipment.


seed S135 document.appendix_1.appendix_2.section_governance_risk
spanId=doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4::section::42887-43935 · chars=[42887, 43935) · scope=unspecified · doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4 · document.appendix_1.appendix_2.section_governance_risk
GOVERNANCE & RISK
Data Center risk management. The AWS Security Operations Center performs regular threat and vulnerability reviews of data centers. Ongoing assessment and mitigation of potential vulnerabilities is performed through data center risk assessment activities. This assessment is performed in addition to the enterprise-level risk assessment process used to identify and manage risks presented to the business as a whole. This process also takes regional regulatory and environmental risks into consideration.
Third-party security attestation. Third-party testing of AWS data centers, as documented in our third-party reports, ensures AWS has appropriately implemented security measures aligned to established rules needed to obtain security certifications. Depending on the compliance program and its requirements, external auditors may perform testing of media disposal, review security camera footage, observe entrances and hallways throughout a data center, test electronic access control devices, and examine data center equipment.

seed S131 document.appendix_1.appendix_2.section_availability
spanId=doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4::section::40382-41181 · chars=[40382, 41181) · scope=unspecified · doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4 · document.appendix_1.appendix_2.section_availability
AVAILABILITY
AWS has identified critical system components required to maintain the availability of our system and recover service in the event of outage. Critical system components are backed up across multiple, isolated locations known as Availability Zones. Each Availability Zone is engineered to operate independently with high reliability. Availability Zones are connected to enable you to easily architect applications that automatically fail-over between Availability Zones without interruption. Highly resilient systems, and therefore service availability, is a function of the system design. Through the use of Availability Zones and data replication, AWS Users can achieve extremely short recovery time and recovery point objectives, as well as the highest levels of service availability.

seed S135 document.appendix_1.appendix_2.section_governance_risk
spanId=doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4::section::42887-43935 · chars=[42887, 43935) · scope=unspecified · doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4 · document.appendix_1.appendix_2.section_governance_risk
GOVERNANCE & RISK
Data Center risk management. The AWS Security Operations Center performs regular threat and vulnerability reviews of data centers. Ongoing assessment and mitigation of potential vulnerabilities is performed through data center risk assessment activities. This assessment is performed in addition to the enterprise-level risk assessment process used to identify and manage risks presented to the business as a whole. This process also takes regional regulatory and environmental risks into consideration.
Third-party security attestation. Third-party testing of AWS data centers, as documented in our third-party reports, ensures AWS has appropriately implemented security measures aligned to established rules needed to obtain security certifications. Depending on the compliance program and its requirements, external auditors may perform testing of media disposal, review security camera footage, observe entrances and hallways throughout a data center, test electronic access control devices, and examine data center equipment.

4 Objection Right for New Sub-processors.
When any new Sub-processor is engaged during the Term, and if the GDPR applies to the processing of Personal Data, Alaio will, at least 10 days before the new Sub-processor processes any Personal Data, inform Administrator of the engagement (including the name and location of the relevant sub-processor and the activities it will perform) by sending an email to the Notification Email Address. Administrator may object to any new Sub-processor by notifying Alaio promptly in writing within five (5) business days after receipt of Alaio notice. In the event Administrator objects to a new Sub-processor, Alaio will use reasonable efforts to make available to Administrator a change in the Services or recommend a commercially reasonable change to Administrator´s configuration or use of the Services to avoid Processing of Personal Data by the objected-to new Sub-processor without unreasonably burdening the User. If Alaio is unable to make available such change within a reasonable period of time, which shall not exceed thirty (30) days, Administrator may terminate the applicable Order with respect only to those Services which cannot be provided by Alaio without the use of the objected-to new Sub-processor by providing written notice to Alaio. Alternative Dispute Resolution Body
In compliance with the EU-U.S. DPF, the UK Extension to the EU-U.S. DPF and the SwissU.S. DPF, Alaio, Inc commits to refer unresolved complaints concerning our handling of personal data received in reliance on the EU-U.S. DPF, the UK Extension to the EU-U.S. DPF and the Swiss-U.S. DPF to JAMS, an alternative dispute resolution provider based in the United States. If you do not receive timely acknowledgment of your DPF Principlesrelated complaint from us, or if we have not addressed your DPF Principles-related complaint to your satisfaction, please visit https://www.jamsadr.com/for more information or to file a complaint.  The services of JAMS are provided at no cost to you.

seed S134 document.appendix_1.appendix_2.section_nfrastructure_maintenance
spanId=doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4::section::42172-42886 · chars=[42172, 42886) · scope=unspecified · doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4 · document.appendix_1.appendix_2.section_nfrastructure_maintenance
NFRASTRUCTURE MAINTENANCE
Equipment maintenance. AWS monitors and performs preventative maintenance of electrical and mechanical equipment to maintain the continued operability of systems within AWS data centers. Equipment maintenance procedures are carried out by qualified persons and completed according to a documented maintenance schedule.
Environment management . AWS monitors electrical and mechanical systems and equipment to enable immediate identification of issues. This is carried out by utilizing continuous audit tools and information provided through our Building Management and Electrical Monitoring Systems. Preventative maintenance is performed to maintain the continued operability of equipment.

DATA PROCESSING AGREEMENT
Last updated 29 th  November 2024
This Data Processing Agreement ('DPA', 'Agreement') is an addendum to and forms part of the master agreement between the Administrator of Bitrix24 Customer Account and Alaio (https://www.bitrix24.com/terms/) to reflect the parties' agreement for the provision of the Processor Services (as amended from time to time) and processing of Bitrix24 Customer Account User's Personal Data in accordance with the requirements of the Data Protection Legislation.
This Data Processing Agreement will be effective from the Effective Date.
BY CLICKING THE "I ACCEPT" BUTTON BELOW, YOU (A) ACKNOWLEDGE THAT YOU HAVE READ AND UNDERSTAND THIS AGREEMENT; (B) REPRESENT AND WARRANT THAT YOU HAVE THE RIGHT, POWER, AND AUTHORITY TO ENTER INTO THIS AGREEMENT; AND (C) ACCEPT THIS AGREEMENT AND AGREE THAT YOU ARE LEGALLY BOUND BY ITS TERMS.

DATA PROCESSING AGREEMENT
Last updated 29 th  November 2024
This Data Processing Agreement ('DPA', 'Agreement') is an addendum to and forms part of the master agreement between the Administrator of Bitrix24 Customer Account and Alaio (https://www.bitrix24.com/terms/) to reflect the parties' agreement for the provision of the Processor Services (as amended from time to time) and processing of Bitrix24 Customer Account User's Personal Data in accordance with the requirements of the Data Protection Legislation.
This Data Processing Agreement will be effective from the Effective Date.
BY CLICKING THE "I ACCEPT" BUTTON BELOW, YOU (A) ACKNOWLEDGE THAT YOU HAVE READ AND UNDERSTAND THIS AGREEMENT; (B) REPRESENT AND WARRANT THAT YOU HAVE THE RIGHT, POWER, AND AUTHORITY TO ENTER INTO THIS AGREEMENT; AND (C) ACCEPT THIS AGREEMENT AND AGREE THAT YOU ARE LEGALLY BOUND BY ITS TERMS.

Alternative Dispute Resolution Body
In compliance with the EU-U.S. DPF, the UK Extension to the EU-U.S. DPF and the SwissU.S. DPF, Alaio, Inc commits to refer unresolved complaints concerning our handling of personal data received in reliance on the EU-U.S. DPF, the UK Extension to the EU-U.S. DPF and the Swiss-U.S. DPF to JAMS, an alternative dispute resolution provider based in the United States. 3. DURATION OF THIS DPA
This DPA will take effect on the Effective Date and, notwithstanding expiry of the Term, remain in effect until, and automatically expire upon, deletion of all User Personal Data by Bitrix24 as described in this DPA.


seed S35 document.clause_4.subclause_4.2
spanId=doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4::section::13363-14205 · chars=[13363, 14205) · scope=unspecified · doc_5e37e897-8070-44c7-aa72-671e085430f4 · document.clause_4.subclause_4.2
4.2 Alaio shall, to the extent legally permitted, promptly notify Administrator if it receives a request from a Data Subject for access to, correction, amendment, restriction, deletion or exercising any other rights under the GDPR or other Data Protection Legislation of that person's Personal Data. Alaio shall not respond to any such Data Subject request without Administrator´s  prior written consent except to confirm that the request relates to User. Alaio shall provide Administrator with cooperation and assistance in relation to handling of a Data Sub ject's request for access to that person's Personal Data or exercising any other rights under the GDPR or other Data Protection Legislation, to the extent legally permitted and to the extent User does not have access to such Personal Data through its use or receipt of the Services.

When any new Sub-processor is engaged during the Term, and if the GDPR applies to the processing of Personal Data, Alaio will, at least 10 days before the new Sub-processor processes any Personal Data, inform Administrator of the engagement (including the name and location of the relevant sub-processor and the activities it will perform) by sending an email to the Notification Email Address. Administrator may object to any new Sub-processor by notifying Alaio promptly in writing within five (5) business days after receipt of Alaio notice.
